Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示，决定模型回复是否有害——生成几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们探讨这些评判是根据回复的内容还是其表述方式进行评分。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前或后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全性的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复中，乘以最多7种形式和8个评判，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“危险”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地操控：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的防护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）是免疫的，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将攻击减少十倍，脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释员的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the effectiveness of automatic safety judges, which assess the harmfulness of model replies, by examining whether their evaluations are based on content or style. Previous methods have relied on direct content analysis, leading to vulnerabilities that can be exploited by altering the presentation of the content without changing its meaning. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that modify the tone of replies while keeping the content unchanged, allowing for a clear assessment of judge reliability. The study employs a comprehensive methodology involving over 600 responses tested against various judges, revealing that while most judges remain stable, certain judges exhibit significant blind spots, with specific wrappers causing notable shifts in verdicts. The findings indicate that the vulnerabilities lie within the judges themselves rather than the content, contributing valuable insights into the robustness of safety evaluation systems and providing tools for further research in this area.
本研究探讨了自动安全评估系统的有效性，这些系统评估模型响应的有害性，并质疑其评估是基于内容还是仅仅基于风格表现。以往的方法依赖于直接的内容评估，这可能通过风格变化而被操控，从而导致安全判断的不一致。提出的方法引入了内容不变的风格包装，这些包装在不改变内容的情况下改变响应的语气，有效地暴露了评估者的脆弱性。该研究采用严格的方法论，测试了超过600个响应与多个评估者的判断翻转率。关键发现表明，尽管大多数评估者保持稳定，但某些评估者表现出显著的盲点，其中一个包装导致GPT-4o-mini的不安全判决翻转率达到19.9%，表明缺陷存在于评估系统中而非内容本身。该论文为理解当前安全评估方法的局限性做出了贡献，并通过发布数据集、包装和代码为进一步研究提供了工具。
The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability
Authors: Xin Xu
First: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing safety monitors for large language models (LLMs), particularly their inability to certify certain 2-safety hyperproperties, which are only observable through two executions. Traditional methods face a binary impossibility, where a single trace cannot determine these properties, leading to a lack of effective monitoring. The proposed approach introduces a graded detectability frontier, replacing binary outcomes with measurable bounds on monitor performance, thus defining an oversight gap that quantifies the shortfall of monitors. The methodology involves analyzing the balanced accuracy of various LLM monitors across different total variation (TV) levels, revealing that while some monitors perform optimally at TV=0, their effectiveness diminishes as TV increases. The findings indicate that a significant portion of the shortfall is not due to capability but rather the information and procedures used in monitoring, with performance metrics showing that monitors average 60.9% accuracy at higher TV levels, which supports the need for improved monitoring strategies.
本文探讨了现有大型语言模型（LLM）安全监控器的局限性，这些监控器在认证复杂属性（如跨租户非干扰和沙袋效应）时，由于其二元决策能力而面临困难。以往的方法往往导致二元不可能性，即单个执行轨迹无法提供足够的信息进行决策。作者提出了一种新方法，用分级可检测性替代二元结果，引入了一个量化监控器在定义阈值以下的短缺的监督差距概念。该方法论涉及分析各种LLM监控器在总变差距离（TV）下的平衡准确性，揭示尽管某些监控器在TV=0时表现最佳，但随着TV的增加，其有效性下降。研究结果表明，性能短缺并不仅仅是由于能力限制，因为特定检查可以显著提高准确性，证明了信息和程序上下文在监控任务中的必要性。所提出的方法在某些配置下达到高达90%的准确率，从而支持了论文提升LLM安全监控目标的实现。
SAEs Can Improve Unlearning: Dynamic Sparse Autoencoder Guardrails for Precision Unlearning in LLMs
Authors: Aashiq Muhamed, Jacopo Bonato, Mona Diab, Virginia Smith
First: 2025-04-11T01:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T03:32:59+00:00
Comments: COLM 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning is a promising approach to improve LLM safety by removing unwanted knowledge from the model. However, prevailing gradient-based unlearning methods suffer from issues such as high computational costs, hyperparameter instability, poor sequential unlearning capability, vulnerability to relearning attacks, low data efficiency, and lack of interpretability. While Sparse Autoencoders are well-suited to improve these aspects by enabling targeted activation-based unlearning, prior approaches underperform gradient-based methods. This work demonstrates that, contrary to these earlier findings, SAEs can significantly improve unlearning when employed dynamically. We introduce $\textbf{Dynamic DAE Guardrails}$ (DSG), a novel method for precision unlearning that leverages principled feature selection and a dynamic classifier. Our experiments show DSG substantially outperforms leading unlearning methods, achieving superior forget-utility trade-offs. DSG addresses key drawbacks of gradient-based approaches for unlearning -- offering enhanced computational efficiency and stability, robust performance in sequential unlearning, stronger resistance to relearning attacks, better data efficiency including zero-shot settings, and more interpretable unlearning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAEs可以改善遗忘：用于LLMs精确遗忘的动态稀疏自编码器护栏
机器遗忘是一种有前景的方法，通过从模型中移除不需要的知识来提高LLM的安全性。然而，现有的基于梯度的遗忘方法存在高计算成本、超参数不稳定、顺序遗忘能力差、易受重学习攻击、数据效率低和缺乏可解释性等问题。稀疏自编码器非常适合通过启用针对性激活的遗忘来改善这些方面，但先前的方法在性能上不如基于梯度的方法。本研究表明，与早期发现相反，SAEs在动态使用时可以显著改善遗忘。我们引入了$\textbf{动态DAE护栏}$（DSG），这是一种利用原则性特征选择和动态分类器的精确遗忘新方法。我们的实验表明，DSG在遗忘方法中显著优于领先的遗忘方法，实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡。DSG解决了基于梯度的遗忘方法的关键缺陷——提供了更高的计算效率和稳定性、在顺序遗忘中的强大性能、对重学习攻击的更强抵抗力、更好的数据效率（包括零样本设置）以及更可解释的遗忘。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenges of machine unlearning in large language models (LLMs), which is essential for enhancing model safety by removing unwanted knowledge. Previous gradient-based unlearning methods face significant limitations, including high computational costs, instability in hyperparameters, and vulnerability to relearning attacks, leading to inefficiencies and poor interpretability. This paper proposes Dynamic DAE Guardrails (DSG), a novel approach that utilizes Sparse Autoencoders to improve unlearning precision through targeted activation and dynamic classification. The methodology demonstrates that DSG significantly outperforms existing unlearning methods, achieving better forget-utility trade-offs and addressing the shortcomings of gradient-based techniques, thus supporting its goals of enhanced efficiency and interpretability in unlearning tasks.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中机器遗忘的挑战，这对于通过消除不必要的知识来增强模型安全性至关重要。以往的基于梯度的遗忘方法受到高计算成本、不稳定性和低效率的困扰，迫切需要改进技术。本文提出了动态稀疏自编码器保护（DSG），这是一种利用动态稀疏自编码器通过有效的特征选择和动态分类器来实现精确遗忘的新方法。该方法表明，DSG显著优于现有的遗忘方法，实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡，并通过提高计算效率、稳定性和可解释性，解决了基于梯度的方法的局限性，同时在顺序遗忘和抵抗重学习攻击方面表现出强大的性能。结果表明，DSG有效地实现了提高LLMs中遗忘性能的目标。
Behind Harmful Compliance: Behavioral and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-05T18:10:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several parameter-level interventions, yet models with matched harmful compliance can exhibit fundamentally different failure modes. We compare harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-feature abliteration in Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B using harmfulness, capability, self-audit, safety reflection, representation similarity, and refusal-direction repair. All three routes reach near-ceiling harmfulness, but SFT causes the broadest capability loss and representational drift; abliteration yields localized, family-dependent refusal-feature suppression; and RLVR largely preserves base-model capability, explicit safety judgments, and representation geometry while retargeting behavior toward compliance. RLVR models consequently remain unusually responsive to safety-reflection prompts despite complying under direct prompting. We further find that harmful RLVR induces capability-blind compliance: models claim to complete unavailable actions and fabricate information about nonexistent entities. Targeted RLVR calibration substantially reduces this false acceptance without restoring safety or degrading general capability. These results show that harmful compliance, harm recognition, and capability awareness are separable behavioral axes, and that self-audit and hallucination patterns are not necessarily robust safety signals under adaptive post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有害合规背后的行为和机制差异：LLM越狱案例分析
开放权重语言模型可以通过多种参数级干预变得不安全，但具有匹配有害合规的模型可能表现出根本不同的失败模式。我们比较了Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B中的有害监督微调（SFT）、有害可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和拒绝特征消融，使用有害性、能力、自我审计、安全反思、表示相似性和拒绝方向修复等指标。所有三种途径都接近极限有害性，但SFT导致最广泛的能力损失和表示漂移；消融产生局部的、依赖于家族的拒绝特征抑制；而RLVR在重新定向合规行为的同时，基本上保留了基础模型的能力、明确的安全判断和表示几何。尽管在直接提示下合规，RLVR模型仍对安全反思提示表现出异常的响应性。我们进一步发现，有害的RLVR会导致能力盲目的合规：模型声称完成不可用的动作，并虚构关于不存在实体的信息。针对性的RLVR校准显著减少了这种错误接受，而不恢复安全或降低一般能力。这些结果表明，有害合规、伤害识别和能力意识是可分离的行为轴，自我审计和幻觉模式在适应性后训练下不一定是稳健的安全信号。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the safety vulnerabilities of open-weight language models, particularly focusing on the different failure modes that arise from various harmful compliance methods. Previous approaches, such as harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), have shown limitations, including significant capability loss and representational drift. The proposed method, RLVR, distinguishes itself by maintaining base-model capabilities while retargeting behavior towards compliance, thus addressing the issues of capability-blind compliance and false acceptance. The paper contributes by demonstrating that harmful compliance and capability awareness are separate behavioral dimensions, and it employs a comparative analysis of SFT, RLVR, and refusal-feature abliteration on models like Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B. The findings reveal that RLVR models can effectively respond to safety-reflection prompts while minimizing false acceptance, achieving a balance between compliance and capability that supports the research goals.
本研究关注开放权重语言模型的安全性问题，特别是不同参数级干预如何导致有害合规的不同失败模式。以往的方法，包括有害的监督微调（SFT）和有害的强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR），显示出能力损失和表征漂移等局限性。所提出的方法RLVR通过在重新定向合规行为的同时保持基础模型能力，解决了能力盲目合规和虚假接受的问题。本文的贡献在于理解有害合规行为的差异及其背后的机制。研究方法涉及比较SFT、RLVR和拒绝特征消融对Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B模型的影响，达到了近乎极限的有害性，同时揭示RLVR模型对安全反思提示的响应能力，表明对语言模型安全性和合规性的理解更加细致。
TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors
Authors: Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi, Hai-Yen Vong, Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung, Tram Ho
First: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIER：评估LLM安全行为的威胁隐含性基准
当前LLM安全基准主要依赖于二元指标，忽视了模型对不同威胁隐含性的有害提示的响应。我们引入TIER，一个用于LLM行为安全评估的威胁隐含性基准。TIER涵盖四个风险领域和四个威胁级别，从明确的有害请求到复杂的越狱。使用六级行为量表和两个独立的LLM评审对响应进行评估。在六个开放权重的LLM上进行的实验表明，安全行为在威胁级别之间逐渐演变，而不是直接从拒绝转变为遵从。上下文提示产生了最丰富的行为，而越狱则揭示了最大的鲁棒性差距。此外，具有相似攻击成功率的模型可能表现出不同的响应分布，突显了对行为意识的LLM安全评估的需求。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety benchmarks, which primarily utilize binary metrics and fail to account for the varying implicitness of threats in harmful prompts. Previous methods have not adequately captured the nuances of model responses, leading to an oversimplified understanding of LLM safety behaviors. The proposed TIER benchmark introduces a more comprehensive evaluation framework that categorizes threats into four risk domains and levels, assessing responses on a six-label behavior scale with independent judges. This methodology reveals that LLM safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels and that contextual prompts elicit a wider range of responses, while jailbreaks expose significant robustness gaps. The findings underscore the necessity for a behavior-aware approach to LLM safety evaluation, demonstrating that models with similar attack success rates can still produce markedly different response patterns, thereby contributing to a more nuanced understanding of LLM safety dynamics.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全基准的局限性，这些基准主要使用二元指标，未能考虑有害提示中威胁的隐含性变化。以往的方法未能有效捕捉模型响应的细微差别，导致对LLM安全行为的理解不完整。提出的TIER基准引入了一种更全面的评估框架，将威胁分为四个级别，并使用六标签行为量表评估响应，从而提供了对LLM安全性更详细的分析。该方法应用于六个开放权重的LLM，揭示了安全行为在威胁级别之间逐渐演变的特点，背景提示产生了最丰富的响应，而越狱攻击则暴露了显著的鲁棒性差距。研究结果强调了在评估LLM安全性时采用行为意识方法的必要性，表明具有相似攻击成功率的模型仍可能表现出不同的响应模式。
IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks
Authors: Saikat Mondal, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu, Asif Ekbal
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T11:46:25+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 33 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Contains examples of harmful model outputs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IndicSafeEval：多语言说服性越狱攻击下大型语言模型的安全鲁棒性
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多语言环境中的使用日益增加，但其安全性仍主要在英语中进行评估。这限制了我们对低资源和文化多样语言中对齐失败表现的理解。我们引入了IndicSafeEval，这是一个针对印度语言的基于说服的越狱评估框架。我们的基准结合了十个安全关键内容类别和六种类人说服策略，涵盖四种不同的印度语言，如印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语，共生成7200个对抗性提示。我们对多个开源LLM进行了系统的黑箱评估，以检查它们的安全行为在不同语言、说服策略和风险类别中的变化。我们的分析表明，模型在所有语言和提示风格中并不表现出同等的安全性。相反，安全性能在很大程度上依赖于所使用的语言和请求的措辞方式。我们进一步观察到，不同的风险类别表现出不同的脆弱性，一些类型的有害内容对基于说服的越狱攻击显著更易受影响。这些发现揭示了当前安全评估的重要局限性，主要集中在英语，并强调了需要多语言和说服意识的基准框架，以更准确地评估现实世界中的LLM安全性。我们的实现可在https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval获取。警告：本文包含可能冒犯或有害的示例数据。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs), which have primarily focused on English, thereby neglecting multilingual contexts and the manifestation of alignment failures in low-resource languages. Previous methods lacked a comprehensive approach to assess safety across diverse languages and persuasive strategies, leading to an incomplete understanding of LLM vulnerabilities. The proposed IndicSafeEval framework introduces a systematic evaluation method that combines ten safety-critical content categories with six persuasive strategies across four Indian languages, generating 7,200 adversarial prompts. This methodology reveals significant variations in safety performance based on language and phrasing, highlighting the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware assessments. The findings indicate that certain risk categories are more vulnerable to persuasion-based attacks, emphasizing the importance of this research in enhancing the safety evaluation of LLMs in diverse linguistic settings.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLMs）安全评估的局限性，这些评估主要集中在英语上，忽视了低资源和文化多样语言中的表现。以往的方法未能充分捕捉不同语言和说服情境下的安全性细微差别，导致对多语言环境中对齐失败的理解不足。提出的IndicSafeEval框架引入了一个综合评估基准，结合了四种印度语言中的各种安全关键内容类别和说服策略，生成了一个包含7200个对抗性提示的强大数据集。该方法论涉及对多个开源LLMs进行系统的黑箱评估，以评估它们在不同语言和风险类别下的安全行为。研究结果表明，安全性能在语言和措辞上存在显著差异，强调了多语言和说服意识安全评估的必要性，从而为更好地理解不同语言环境中LLM的脆弱性做出了贡献。
FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models
Authors: Yalun Wu, Junfeng Fang, Jiawei Wang, Haotian Liu, Qijun Yang, Minghan Yang, Hongcheng Guo, Zhoujun Li, Boyang Wang
Venue: Proceedings of the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM), 2026
First: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLY-EVAL++：一种基于证据的安全约束飞行预测评估协议
在安全关键、物理约束的环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）需要的不仅仅是基于准确性的指标，因为数值上接近真实值的预测仍然可能违反操作约束、以物理不一致的方式组合领域，或未能产生可用的结构化输出。现有的评估协议无法可靠地测量这些失败模式。我们提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据的评估协议，结合了对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全约束的确定性验证，以及通过固定评分标准引导的聚合，形成可解释的多维评分。我们通过扩展PilotBench设置，结合历史条件和多步预测任务，为飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）实例化FLY-EVAL++。在66个LLM中，安全合规性是模型行为中最具区分性的维度：具有可比预测性能的模型在安全评分上相差超过28分，我们观察到反复出现的失败，包括在物理上合理的预测下的安全违规和多步展开中的不稳定性。这些结果表明，在安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足和结构有效性，而不是仅依赖于以准确性为中心的报告。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for effective evaluation protocols for large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical environments, where traditional accuracy-based metrics fail to capture operational constraints and physical feasibility. Previous methods inadequately measure failure modes such as safety violations and inconsistencies in predictions, prompting the development of FLY-EVAL++, which integrates deterministic verification of compliance with safety constraints and interpretable multi-dimensional scoring. This paper contributes by establishing a robust evaluation framework specifically for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP), demonstrating that safety compliance is a critical differentiator among models, with significant performance variations observed even among those with similar predictive accuracy. The methodology involves extending the PilotBench setting to include history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks, revealing that explicit measurement of constraint satisfaction and structured validity is essential for evaluating models in safety-critical domains.

TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-01T03:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：基于轨迹的候选选择用于LLM越狱后缀优化
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选者来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选者往往在搜索的后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明，候选选择而非仅仅候选生成是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的基于轨迹的候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选者的即时损失来选择候选者，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which typically select candidates based solely on the lowest current loss, leading to suboptimal long-term outcomes due to myopic decision-making. The proposed TACS framework introduces a trajectory-aware candidate selection process that enhances per-step evaluations with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selections through reference-policy regularization and a chi-squared correction, effectively mitigating the issues of selection-stage reward hacking. This approach is well-motivated as it targets a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. The methodology was tested on the HarmBench task, where TACS demonstrated superior performance compared to strong baselines, achieving higher attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior, thus supporting the goal of improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
本研究解决了基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常仅根据候选者的即时损失进行选择，导致由于选择阶段奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。提出的TACS框架通过引入一种轨迹感知的候选选择过程来有所不同，该过程不仅根据当前表现评估候选者，还考虑其未来的有效性，利用参考策略正则化和卡方修正来稳定选择过程。本文的贡献在于证明解决候选选择的隐性瓶颈可以显著提高优化结果。通过在HarmBench上的实验，TACS在攻击成功率和稳定性方面明显优于现有方法，表明所提出的方法有效支持其优化越狱后缀选择的目标。
Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Qilong Wu, Sahil Wadhwa, Pranab Mohanty, Giri Iyengar, Varun Chandrasekaran
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00
Comments: To appear on EMNLP 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对大型语言模型的有效性感知越狱评估
越狱鲁棒性已成为大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的核心，但现有方法主要依赖于拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配启发式，这些方法强调语言的合理性而非正确性。我们识别出现有评估的一个关键限制：许多越狱意图依赖于指令有效性而非认知事实性，允许看似真实的响应被标记为成功，尽管在事实或程序上是错误的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了顺序认知和行动级验证（SEAV），这是一个以验证为中心的越狱评估框架，它将响应分解为有序步骤，并评估有效性和正确性。SEAV结合了LLM作为评判机制的语义解释与使用外部知识源的检索基础验证，评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上连贯，并在操作上能够推进有害目标。实证结果表明，SEAV将SD-A（一个策划的战略不诚实诊断）的假阳性率降低了14.9个百分点，相较于最强基线，并在四个公共基准中的三个重新将22.1%至51.0%的先前标记成功样本重新分类为无效。这些结果表明，强制正确性显著重塑了测量的鲁棒性：许多先前标记的越狱成功被重新分类为无效，且结果在测试的搜索后端和评估模型中保持稳定。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical issue of jailbreak robustness in large language models (LLMs), highlighting limitations in existing evaluation methods that focus on linguistic plausibility rather than factual correctness. Previous methodologies primarily relied on refusal behavior and intent-matching heuristics, which often misclassify responses as successful despite their factual inaccuracies. The proposed Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV) framework offers a novel approach by decomposing responses into ordered steps and evaluating both validity and correctness, integrating LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms with retrieval-grounded verification. This methodology significantly improves evaluation accuracy, as demonstrated by a 14.9 percentage point reduction in false positives on the SD-A diagnostic and a reclassification of 22.1% to 51.0% of previously labeled successes as invalid across multiple benchmarks. These findings indicate that prioritizing correctness can fundamentally alter the assessment of jailbreak robustness in LLMs.

Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services
Authors: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
First: 2026-03-11T14:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T14:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM safety evaluations rely on binary attack-success rates and domain-agnostic taxonomies, leaving regulated Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) deployments exposed to failures elicited through legally or professionally plausible framing. We introduce RAHS (Risk-Adjusted Harm Score), a risk-sensitive metric jointly capturing disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, and FinRedTeamBench, a 989-prompt benchmark spanning seven BFSI risk areas and 34 sub-categories mapped to regulatory frameworks. Evaluation uses an ensemble of three heterogeneous LLM judges, validated against human experts, and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline. On nine open-weight models, RAHS preserves separation under near-ceiling ASR, ranking is stable under hyperparameter sweeps, and multi-turn pressure drives not only more jailbreaks but more operationally severe disclosures, exposing failure modes that single-turn, domain-agnostic evaluations cannot reveal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：金融服务中针对大型语言模型的自动红队风险调整危害评分
现有的大型语言模型安全评估依赖于二元攻击成功率和领域无关的分类法，使受监管的银行、金融服务和保险（BFSI）部署面临通过合法或专业上合理的框架引发的失败。我们引入了RAHS（风险调整危害评分），这是一种风险敏感的指标，联合捕捉披露严重性、免责声明缓解和评审者间一致性，以及FinRedTeamBench，这是一个涵盖七个BFSI风险领域和34个子类别的989个提示基准，映射到监管框架。评估使用三位异构大型语言模型评审者的集成，经过与人类专家的验证，以及自适应的多轮红队流程。在九个开放权重模型上，RAHS在接近上限的攻击成功率下保持分离，排名在超参数搜索中稳定，多轮压力不仅驱动更多的越狱行为，还导致更具操作严重性的披露，暴露出单轮、领域无关评估无法揭示的失败模式。

The research addresses the inadequacies of existing safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs) in the Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) sectors, which often rely on binary success rates and generic taxonomies, leaving these systems vulnerable to nuanced failures. The proposed Risk-Adjusted Harm Score (RAHS) improves upon past methods by incorporating a risk-sensitive metric that evaluates disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, thus providing a more comprehensive assessment tailored to regulatory frameworks. This paper contributes a novel benchmark, FinRedTeamBench, consisting of 989 prompts across seven BFSI risk areas, and employs an ensemble of three diverse LLM judges validated against human experts within an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming framework. Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-15T14:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T11:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating data remains a costly bottleneck for supervised NLP. Active learning (AL) reduces the number of human labels needed by selecting only the most informative instances, while instruction-tuned LLMs attack the same bottleneck from the other side, making labels cheap enough to annotate entire corpora. This raises two questions: can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be cheaply labeled? We investigate both by training supervised hate speech classifiers on a new dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, comparing human and LLM annotation under matched conditions. LLM annotation at scale outperforms human-supervised classifiers at roughly one-tenth the cost, for both a closed-source (GPT-5.2) and an open-weight (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) LLM, and the advantage is robust under soft-label evaluation. It hinges on the annotation interface: only a two-question decomposition mirroring the human annotation task unlocks it. AL provides no reliable advantage over random sampling in our prefiltered pool. Error structure depends on the LLM: only GPT-5.2 matches the human FP/FN balance, while other variants over-flag border-control and economic-competition discourse. Humans remain essential as evaluators; for training labels, the question shifts to which LLM, which interface, and what shape of pool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们还需要人类参与吗？在TikTok仇恨言论检测中人类与LLM注释的主动学习比较
数据注释仍然是监督NLP的一个成本瓶颈。主动学习（AL）通过仅选择最具信息量的实例来减少所需的人类标签数量，而经过指令调优的LLM则从另一个角度解决同一瓶颈，使标签变得足够便宜以注释整个语料库。这引发了两个问题：LLM标签能否在AL循环中替代人类标签，以及当整个语料库可以被廉价标注时，AL是否仍然必要？我们通过在一个包含278K条德国政治TikTok评论的新数据集上训练监督仇恨言论分类器来研究这两个问题，比较在匹配条件下的人类和LLM注释。大规模LLM注释的表现优于人类监督的分类器，成本约为十分之一，适用于封闭源（GPT-5.2）和开放权重（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）LLM，并且在软标签评估下优势稳健。这依赖于注释接口：只有与人类注释任务相对应的两个问题的分解才能解锁它。在我们预过滤的池中，AL对随机抽样没有可靠的优势。错误结构依赖于LLM：只有GPT-5.2匹配人类的FP/FN平衡，而其他变体则过度标记边境控制和经济竞争话语。人类作为评估者仍然至关重要；对于训练标签，问题转向哪个LLM、哪个接口以及什么形状的池。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of costly data annotation in supervised natural language processing (NLP), particularly in the context of hate speech detection on TikTok. Previous methods relied heavily on human annotation or traditional active learning (AL) techniques, which have limitations in efficiency and scalability. This paper proposes an innovative approach that leverages instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) to generate labels at a significantly lower cost while questioning the necessity of human involvement in the annotation loop. The study employs a new dataset of 278,000 German political TikTok comments to train hate speech classifiers, comparing the performance of human versus LLM annotations. The findings reveal that LLM annotations outperform human annotations at approximately one-tenth of the cost, although AL does not provide a reliable advantage over random sampling in this context, indicating that while humans are still needed for evaluation, the focus should shift towards optimizing LLM selection and annotation interfaces.
本研究解决了监督自然语言处理（NLP）中数据标注的挑战，特别是在TikTok仇恨言论检测的背景下，传统方法严重依赖人工标注，导致高成本和低效率。以往的方法利用主动学习（AL）通过选择最具信息量的实例来减少人工标注，但本研究质疑在大型语言模型（LLMs）能够提供廉价标注时，人工参与的必要性。所提出的方法比较了在278K条德国政治TikTok评论数据集上训练分类器时，人工标注和LLM标注的效果，结果显示LLM标注在成本仅为人工标注十分之一的情况下显著优于人工标注，尤其是在使用特定标注界面时。研究结果表明，尽管AL没有提供可靠的优势，但LLM可以有效替代人工标注，尽管人类在评估过程中仍然至关重要，因此研究重点转向选择合适的LLM和标注策略以获得最佳结果。
From Detection to Refusal: Safer LLMs via Circuit-Guided Weight Scaling
Authors: Kuan-Lin Chu, Chung-En Sun, Tsui-Wei Weng
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the 2026 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing (EMNLP 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从检测到拒绝：通过电路引导的权重缩放实现更安全的LLM
尽管进行了广泛的对齐努力，大型语言模型（LLM）在对抗性提示下仍然容易生成不安全内容，但实现安全行为的内部机制仍然不够清楚。我们从机制可解释性的角度研究LLM安全性，并描述一个多阶段的*安全电路*，该电路组织拒绝行为，包括（i）响应有害输入的$\textbf{有害检测头}$，（ii）在残差流中介导和稳定安全信号的$\textbf{安全神经元}$，以及（iii）将这些信号转化为安全响应生成的$\textbf{拒绝头}$。通过针对性注意头和神经元级干预，我们提供了与该电路组织一致的因果证据，表明抑制上游有害检测头会干扰下游拒绝行为，而安全神经元介导了这种互动。我们验证了这种分解在多个LLM架构和对抗攻击设置中反复出现，并使用简单的、保持架构的权重缩放作为机制探针来测试其功能相关性。在六个LLM中，电路引导的缩放在攻击下提高了26.5%的安全率，同时在四个标准基准上仅造成1.7%的准确率下降。总体而言，我们的结果支持LLM安全性的电路级解释，并表明机制抽象可以揭示对齐行为背后的稳定和可转移模式。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the ongoing vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generating unsafe content despite alignment efforts, highlighting the need for a better understanding of the mechanisms behind safety behaviors. Previous methods have not effectively elucidated these internal mechanisms, leading to a lack of clarity in how safety is achieved. The proposed approach introduces a multi-stage safety circuit that includes Harmful Detection Heads, Safety Neurons, and Refusal Heads, which collectively enhance the understanding of refusal behavior in LLMs. By employing targeted interventions and circuit-guided weight scaling, the study demonstrates that this method significantly improves safety rates under adversarial conditions by 26.5% while only slightly reducing accuracy by 1.7% across standard benchmarks. The findings contribute to a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety, indicating that mechanistic insights can uncover stable patterns in aligned behavior.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在对抗性提示下生成不安全内容的持续脆弱性，强调了深入理解安全行为机制的必要性。以往的方法主要集中在对齐上，但未能充分解决管理拒绝行为的内部过程，导致在对抗环境中的安全性不足。提出的方法引入了一个多阶段安全电路，包括有害检测头、安全神经元和拒绝头，这些组件共同增强了对LLM安全性的理解。通过采用针对性的干预和电路引导的权重缩放，研究表明抑制有害检测可以干扰拒绝行为，而安全神经元在其中起着关键的中介作用。该方法论显示，电路引导的缩放在六个LLM中在对抗攻击下提高了26.5%的安全率，同时在标准基准测试中仅造成1.7%的准确率下降，从而支持了LLM安全的电路级解释，并揭示了对齐行为中的可转移模式。
Turning the Spell Around: Lightweight Alignment Amplification via Rank-One Safety Injection
Authors: Harethah Abu Shairah, Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud, George Turkiyyah, Bernard Ghanem
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2025-08-28T13:22:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T07:43:32+00:00
Comments: EMNLP 2026, Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) often involves mediating internal representations to refuse harmful requests. Recent research has demonstrated that these safety mechanisms can be bypassed by ablating or removing specific representational directions within the model. In this paper, we propose the opposite approach: Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI), a white-box method that amplifies a model's safety alignment by permanently steering its activations toward the refusal-mediating subspace. ROSI operates as a simple, fine-tuning-free rank-one weight modification applied to all residual stream write matrices. The required safety direction can be computed from a small set of harmful and harmless instruction pairs. We show that ROSI consistently increases safety refusal rates - as evaluated by Llama Guard 3 - while preserving the utility of the model on standard benchmarks such as MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. Furthermore, we show that ROSI can also re-align 'uncensored' models by amplifying their own latent safety directions, demonstrating its utility as an effective last-mile safety procedure. Our results suggest that targeted, interpretable weight steering is a cheap and potent mechanism to improve LLM safety, complementing more resource-intensive fine-tuning paradigms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扭转咒语：通过秩一安全注入实现轻量级对齐放大
大型语言模型（LLMs）中的安全对齐通常涉及调解内部表示以拒绝有害请求。最近的研究表明，这些安全机制可以通过消除或移除模型内特定的表示方向来绕过。在本文中，我们提出了相反的方法：秩一安全注入（ROSI），这是一种白盒方法，通过永久性地将模型的激活引导到拒绝调解子空间来放大模型的安全对齐。ROSI作为一种简单的、无需微调的秩一权重修改，应用于所有残差流写矩阵。所需的安全方向可以从一小组有害和无害的指令对中计算得出。我们展示了ROSI在评估Llama Guard 3时始终提高安全拒绝率，同时在MMLU、HellaSwag和Arc等标准基准上保持模型的实用性。此外，我们还展示了ROSI可以通过放大自身潜在安全方向来重新对齐“未审查”模型，证明其作为有效的最后一公里安全程序的实用性。我们的结果表明，针对性的、可解释的权重引导是一种廉价而有效的机制，可以提高LLM的安全性，补充更资源密集的微调范式。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs), which often fail to refuse harmful requests due to vulnerabilities in their internal representations. Previous methods have focused on mediating these representations but can be circumvented by altering specific directions within the model. The proposed Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI) method differs by amplifying safety alignment through a straightforward, fine-tuning-free modification of weight matrices, steering activations toward a refusal-mediating subspace. This approach is well-motivated as it enhances safety without compromising model utility, achieving increased safety refusal rates as evaluated by Llama Guard 3 while maintaining performance on standard benchmarks like MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. The paper contributes a novel and efficient technique for improving LLM safety, demonstrating that targeted weight adjustments can effectively enhance safety mechanisms in a cost-effective manner.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中安全对齐的挑战，现有方法常常因有害请求能够通过改变内部表示而绕过安全机制而失效。所提出的方法，秩一安全注入（ROSI），与过去的方法不同，它通过增强安全对齐而不是试图调解有害请求，利用简单的秩一权重修改将模型激活引导至安全子空间。该方法的动机充分，因为它提供了一种轻量级的解决方案，以增强安全性，而无需大量微调。本文的贡献在于证明ROSI显著提高了安全拒绝率，使用Llama Guard 3进行测量，同时保持模型在标准基准（如MMLU、HellaSwag和Arc）上的性能。结果表明，ROSI有效地作为LLMs的最后一公里安全增强，提供了一种成本效益高的替代传统微调技术的方法。
Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Junwei Yao, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Wenbo Ye, Jiaxi Wen, Lai Yun Choi
First: 2026-08-01T22:28:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T05:09:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Sci-FM Workshop @ COLM 2026 (non-archival). Workshop version with reviews: https://openreview.net/forum?id=mZh0MqpOOC
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：单一规范提示低估了大型语言模型安全性的表面形式敏感性
基准分数是一种测量工具，但大多数基准以单一规范表面形式读取每个项目。我们询问这种读取是否忠实：当项目的意图保持不变，仅其保持意义的表面形式变化时，规范形式分数是否能很好地估计模型行为，以及任何变化中有多少是解码/评判噪声而非信号？我们在安全性这一高风险环境中实例化这一点，因其没有金标准可供平均。为了避免先前的混淆，我们预先授权了重构（无拒绝，主要非大型语言模型：机器回译和矩阵语言框架代码切换生成器），以便相同的表面形式到达每个模型，使用一个人类锚定的、中立的评审（Claude，kappa = 0.86与人类在不安全合规性上的比较，在各语言中稳定，由GPT-4o交叉验证），并验证意图保持。在370个种子x 5个表面形式x 5个模型中，没有单一转换是普遍最危险的（20个每个转换的McNemar测试中有6个在校正后存活，大多数是保护性的）。然而，仅评估规范提示低估了不安全合规性：跨形式的不安全结果的并集甚至超过了最糟糕的单一形式3.3-12.9个百分点，所有五个模型的自助法95%置信区间均不包括零，且5-13%的在规范下安全的种子在某些重构下是不安全的——高于零随机性底线（规范在温度0下重采样五次给出0/370个新暴露）。这一差距的大小依赖于模型（在Gemini 2.5 Pro上最大）。一种形式仅恢复了模型观察到的不安全表面的约53%，大约三种形式达到了85%——这是对这一形式集的冗余特征，而非定义人口的特征。一个良性对照（XSTest）表明不稳定性是双向的，尽管良性和有害池并未按项目匹配。我们发布了数据集、代码和每个响应的标签。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the effectiveness of benchmark scores in evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety contexts, where traditional methods often rely on a single canonical surface form, potentially leading to inaccurate assessments of model behavior. Previous approaches have not accounted for variations in surface forms, which can obscure the true safety performance of models. The proposed method involves pre-authoring multiple reformulations of prompts to ensure consistent intent while varying surface forms, and evaluating responses using a human-anchored judge to mitigate noise. The study reveals that relying solely on canonical prompts underestimates unsafe compliance, with findings indicating that the total unsafe outcomes across different forms significantly exceed those from any single form. The methodology was tested on 370 seeds across five models, demonstrating that the proposed approach effectively captures a broader range of unsafe behaviors, thus contributing valuable insights into LLM safety evaluation.
本研究解决了现有评估大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性基准的局限性，这些基准通常依赖单一的规范表面形式进行评分。以往的方法未能充分捕捉到模型行为在表面形式变化时的变异性，导致对不安全合规性的潜在低估。所提出的方法涉及预先编写多种提示的改写，以确保意图一致的同时变化表面形式，并通过人类评估者对响应进行评分，以减少噪声。研究发现，仅评估规范提示显著低估了不安全结果，不同形式的不安全结果的联合超过了最差单一形式，差距显著。该方法论包括对370个种子在五种表面形式和五个模型上进行测试，揭示没有单一改写形式始终是最危险的，并强调安全评估的模型依赖性。研究结果通过展示表面形式敏感性的重要性，为更好地理解LLM安全性做出了贡献，并发布了数据集以供进一步研究。
NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation
Authors: Zhiyuan Xu, Muhammad Firhard Roslan, Joseph Gardiner, Sana Belguith, Lichao Wu
First: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome. This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronFuzz：安全神经元引导的LLM安全评估模糊测试
安全评估对于评估对齐的大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能够抵御越狱攻击至关重要。然而，现有的自动化测试方法主要依赖于响应级反馈：每个候选提示通常需要生成目标模型的响应以评估其攻击有效性。这个过程成本高昂，更重要的是，对于大多数候选者在强对齐模型中被拒绝的情况下，仅提供稀疏的指导。 本文提出了NeuronFuzz，一个利用内部安全神经元作为LLM安全评估的连续执行反馈的白盒模糊测试框架。SafetyOracle将安全神经元的激活转换为连续的安全警报分数，作为模糊测试的反馈，并且可以在预填充期间获得，从而消除模糊测试循环中的响应生成。为了构建SafetyOracle，NeuronFuzz使用模板不变的有害和良性输入以及稳定性感知选择，识别出一组紧凑的安全神经元，其激活捕捉有害意图识别。此外，由于安全警报分数是可微分的，NeuronFuzz利用其梯度识别安全敏感的模板位置，并使用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害负载并避免额外的优化变量。我们在21个文本和多模态模型上评估了NeuronFuzz。在五个白盒源模型中，它实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，超越基线最多48个百分点。其优化模板进一步零-shot转移到开放权重和六个专有目标模型，分别实现69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%的平均ASR和前5集成ASR（EASR）。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for effective safety evaluation of aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, highlighting the limitations of existing automated testing methods that rely on response-level feedback, which is costly and provides limited guidance. The proposed approach, NeuronFuzz, introduces a white-box fuzzing framework that utilizes internal safety neurons for continuous execution feedback, thereby eliminating the need for response generation in the fuzzing process. This method is well-motivated as it aims to enhance the robustness of LLMs by providing a more efficient evaluation mechanism. NeuronFuzz constructs a SafetyOracle that translates safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score, allowing for the identification of safety-sensitive template positions and the generation of context-compatible mutations. The framework was evaluated across 21 text and multimodal models, achieving a jailbreak discovery rate of 76-100% in five white-box source models, significantly outperforming existing baselines by up to 48 percentage points, thus demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing LLM safety evaluation.
本研究针对对齐的大型语言模型（LLMs）在抵御越狱攻击方面的有效安全评估的迫切需求，指出现有依赖响应级反馈的自动化测试方法的局限性，这些方法成本高且对强对齐模型提供的指导有限。提出的NeuronFuzz框架通过利用内部安全神经元提供连续执行反馈，从而消除了在模糊测试过程中生成响应的需求，体现了其独特性。这种方法是合理的，因为它通过将神经元激活转换为连续的安全警报分数，能够更高效地识别有害意图。该方法论涉及使用模板不变的输入和稳定性感知选择来确定一组紧凑的安全神经元，NeuronFuzz在五个白盒模型上实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，并且在性能上比以前的方法提高了最多48个百分点。此外，NeuronFuzz在不同模型上的有效可转移性表现出色，平均攻击成功率分别为69.6%和44.1%.
'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：'翻译中的隐秘'即未见的危害：使用'乌尔都语遗漏'分数测量跨脚本安全性不一致性在LLM仇恨言论检测中的应用
乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都语-英语。在五个乌尔都语脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）到31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%到9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the significant gap in safety evaluation for Urdu, a language with 246 million speakers, in the context of large language models (LLMs), which has been largely overlooked in past studies. Previous methods failed to adequately assess the reliability of content moderation across different scripts, leading to inconsistencies in harm detection. The proposed approach systematically evaluates five LLMs across multiple datasets, revealing substantial label instability and a notable 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, indicating that harmful content is often misclassified when translated from Urdu to English. This study contributes to the understanding of cross-script safety inconsistencies in LLMs and highlights the urgent need for inclusive evaluation practices. The methodology involves testing LLMs on Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English datasets, demonstrating that current models exhibit uneven safety assurance, particularly with smaller open-weight models showing higher instability and missed harm rates.
本研究关注乌尔都语在大型语言模型（LLMs）安全评估中的显著缺口，这一广泛使用的语言在以往研究和会议中几乎被忽视。过去的方法未能充分评估不同脚本间内容审核的可靠性，导致危害检测的不一致性。本研究通过评估五个LLMs在Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语和英语多个数据集上的表现，提出了一种新方法，揭示了显著的标签不稳定性和明显的“漏检乌尔都”率，表明在原始脚本中未能检测到的有害内容。研究结果有助于理解LLMs中的跨脚本安全不一致性，表明当前模型在乌尔都语中表现出不均匀的安全保障，尤其是较小的模型漏检危害的比率更高。该方法论涉及在多样化数据集上测试LLMs，取得了对其性能的重大见解，并强调了改善乌尔都语处理安全措施的迫切需求。
ADVERSA: Measuring Multi-Turn Guardrail Degradation and Judge Reliability in Large Language Models
Authors: Harry Owiredu-Ashley
Venue: 2026 IEEE/ACIS 24th International Conference on Software Engineering Research, Management and Applications (SERA), Towson, MD, USA, 2026, pp. 414-417
First: 2026-03-10T03:00:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T05:47:14+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 12 figures. Independent research. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/Harry-Ashley/adversa-guardrail-degradation. Published in the 2026 IEEE/ACIS 24th International Conference on Software Engineering Research, Management and Applications (SERA), pp. 414-417. Recipient of the Best Paper Award at IEEE/ACIS SERA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Most adversarial evaluations of large language model (LLM) safety assess single prompts and report binary pass/fail outcomes, which fails to capture how safety properties evolve under sustained adversarial interaction. We present ADVERSA, an automated red-teaming framework that measures guardrail degradation dynamics as continuous per-round compliance trajectories rather than discrete jailbreak events. ADVERSA uses a fine-tuned 70B attacker model (ADVERSA-Red, Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct with QLoRA) that eliminates the attacker-side safety refusals that render off-the-shelf models unreliable as attackers, scoring victim responses on a structured 5-point rubric that treats partial compliance as a distinct measurable state. We report a controlled experiment across three frontier victim models (Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.2) using a triple-judge consensus architecture in which judge reliability is measured as a first-class research outcome rather than assumed. Across 15 conversations of up to 10 adversarial rounds, we observe a 26.7% jailbreak rate with an average jailbreak round of 1.25, suggesting that in this evaluation setting, successful jailbreaks were concentrated in early rounds rather than accumulating through sustained pressure. We document inter-judge agreement rates, self-judge scoring tendencies, attacker drift as a failure mode in fine-tuned attackers deployed out of their training distribution, and attacker refusals as a previously-underreported confound in victim resistance measurement. All limitations are stated explicitly. Attack prompts are withheld per responsible disclosure policy; all other experimental artifacts are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ADVERSA：测量大型语言模型中的多轮护栏退化和评审可靠性
大多数针对大型语言模型（LLM）安全性的对抗性评估仅评估单个提示并报告二元通过/失败结果，这未能捕捉安全属性在持续对抗交互下的演变。我们提出了ADVERSA，一个自动化红队框架，测量护栏退化动态，作为连续的每轮合规轨迹，而不是离散的越狱事件。ADVERSA使用经过微调的70B攻击者模型（ADVERSA-Red，Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct与QLoRA），消除了攻击者侧的安全拒绝，使现成模型在攻击者中不可靠，使用结构化的5分制评分受害者响应，将部分合规视为一个独特的可测量状态。我们报告了在三个前沿受害者模型（Claude Opus 4.6，Gemini 3.1 Pro，GPT-5.2）上进行的受控实验，使用三重评审共识架构，其中评审可靠性被视为一项一流的研究结果，而不是假设。在15次对话中，最多进行10轮对抗，我们观察到26.7%的越狱率，平均越狱轮次为1.25，这表明在该评估环境中，成功的越狱集中在早期轮次，而不是通过持续压力累积。我们记录了评审间一致性率、自我评审评分倾向、在超出其训练分布的微调攻击者中作为失败模式的攻击者漂移，以及作为受害者抵抗测量中先前未报告的混淆因素的攻击者拒绝。所有限制均明确说明。攻击提示根据负责任的披露政策被保留；所有其他实验材料均已发布。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the limitations of existing adversarial evaluations of large language models (LLMs), which typically assess safety through single prompts and binary outcomes, failing to capture the dynamics of safety properties during prolonged adversarial interactions. The proposed ADVERSA framework introduces a novel automated red-teaming approach that measures guardrail degradation as continuous compliance trajectories rather than discrete events, utilizing a fine-tuned 70B attacker model to enhance reliability in scoring victim responses. The study contributes to the field by systematically measuring judge reliability and documenting various failure modes, including attacker drift and refusals, through controlled experiments involving three advanced victim models across multiple adversarial rounds. The findings reveal a 26.7% jailbreak rate, indicating that successful breaches predominantly occur in early rounds, thus supporting the framework's goal of providing a more nuanced understanding of LLM safety dynamics.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）对抗性评估的局限性，这些评估通常通过单一提示和二元结果来评估安全性，未能捕捉在长期对抗交互中安全属性的动态变化。提出的ADVERSA框架引入了对护栏退化的连续测量，使用经过微调的70B攻击者模型，通过消除阻碍现成模型的安全拒绝来提高可靠性。本文的贡献在于其创新的方法，将评审者的可靠性作为主要结果进行评估，并采用结构化评分系统来识别部分合规性。该方法涉及对三个先进受害者模型的控制实验，揭示了在多个对抗回合中26.7%的越狱率，表明脆弱性在早期交互中更为明显，而不是随着时间的推移而累积。这一表现支持了在持续对抗压力下提供对LLM安全性更细致理解的目标。
NeuronGuard: Robust LLM Safety Alignment via Ablation-Aware Safety Signal Redistribution
Authors: Anjun Gao, Yueyang Quan, Yufei Xia, Zhuqing Liu, Minghong Fang
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-25T01:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T01:36:54+00:00
Comments: To appear in EMNLP 2026 (Findings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) remains brittle against a growing spectrum of attacks. Jailbreak attacks bypass safety mechanisms through crafted prompts, while neuron-level attacks directly prune safety-critical neurons post-deployment. Both exploit a common weakness: safety-relevant information concentrates in a sparse neuron subset. We present NeuronGuard, a fine-tuning-stage defense that simultaneously hardens LLMs against both attack classes by redistributing safety signals across a broader set of neurons. NeuronGuard dynamically identifies safety-critical neurons via periodically refreshed per-layer linear classifiers, forces refusal behavior under deliberate neuron ablation, and applies KL-divergence regularization for distributional consistency. A randomized gradient projection strategy preserves downstream task utility by resolving conflicts between the defense and task objectives. We provide a formal guarantee that NeuronGuard strictly reduces the attack success rate (ASR) upper bound, and experiments across three LLMs, six state-of-the-art attack strategies, and multimodal settings confirm near-zero ASR while maintaining task accuracy, including against white-box adaptive adversaries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronGuard：通过消融感知安全信号重分配实现稳健的LLM安全对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全对齐在面对日益增长的攻击谱时仍然脆弱。越狱攻击通过精心设计的提示绕过安全机制，而神经元级攻击则在部署后直接修剪安全关键神经元。两者都利用了一个共同的弱点：与安全相关的信息集中在稀疏的神经元子集中。我们提出了NeuronGuard，这是一种微调阶段的防御方法，通过在更广泛的神经元集上重新分配安全信号，来同时增强LLMs对这两类攻击的抵御能力。NeuronGuard通过定期更新的每层线性分类器动态识别安全关键神经元，在故意消融神经元时强制拒绝行为，并应用KL散度正则化以保持分布一致性。一种随机梯度投影策略通过解决防御与任务目标之间的冲突，保持下游任务的效用。我们提供了一个正式保证，NeuronGuard严格降低了攻击成功率（ASR）的上限，且在三种LLM、六种最先进的攻击策略和多模态设置下的实验确认了接近零的ASR，同时保持任务准确性，包括对抗白盒自适应对手的情况。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of large language models (LLMs) to various safety attacks, including jailbreak and neuron-level attacks, which exploit the concentration of safety-relevant information in a limited number of neurons. Previous methods have struggled to provide robust defenses against these attacks, leading to a need for improved safety alignment strategies. The proposed NeuronGuard approach enhances safety by redistributing safety signals across a wider array of neurons, utilizing dynamic identification of safety-critical neurons and applying KL-divergence regularization to ensure consistency. This methodology not only guarantees a reduction in the attack success rate but also maintains task performance, achieving near-zero attack success rates across multiple LLMs and attack strategies while preserving accuracy in downstream tasks.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）在面对各种安全攻击时的脆弱性，特别是越狱攻击和神经元级攻击，这些攻击利用了安全相关信息集中在有限神经元子集中的弱点。以往的方法在这一问题上表现不佳，往往无法同时提供对这两种攻击的强有力防御。提出的NeuronGuard方法通过在更广泛的神经元中重新分配安全信号来增强安全对齐，采用动态识别安全关键神经元，并利用KL散度正则化来保持一致性。该方法在多个模型和攻击策略上实现了攻击成功率的显著降低，同时保持了LLMs在下游任务上的性能，达到了近零的攻击成功率，从而支持了改善LLMs安全对齐的目标。
Register Shifts Break LLM Safety: A Bengali Benchmark with Culturally Grounded Harms
Authors: Naymul Islam, Nusrat Jahan Lia, Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Sabik Bin Sultan, Abdullah Khan Zehady
First: 2026-08-23T10:09:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-23T10:09:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bengali is the seventh-most-spoken language globally, yet LLM safety evaluation remains overwhelmingly English-centric. We introduce BanglaSafe, a benchmark of 879 Bengali prompts combining 309 natively authored prompts with 570 expert-reviewed prompts, spanning 17 culturally grounded harm categories and five prompting conditions that vary language, writing style, and authority framing. Evaluating 18 frontier LLMs, we find that over half of all responses are unsafe or partially unsafe (53.6%) while 14.7% contains strictly harmful content, and that the strongest observed effect is not the switch from English to Bengali but the choice of writing style within Bengali: the same harmful request phrased as a formal newspaper investigation succeeds 17 percentage points more often than the same request phrased as a casual message, with no adversarial engineering involved. We further show that existing safety classifiers struggle to reliably evaluate Bengali content, with even frontier models failing on nearly half of all cases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注册班次突破LLM安全：一个具有文化基础危害的孟加拉基准
孟加拉语是全球第七大语言，但LLM安全评估仍然以英语为中心。我们推出了BanglaSafe，这是一个包含879个孟加拉提示的基准，结合了309个本土创作的提示和570个专家审核的提示，涵盖17个文化基础的危害类别和五种变化语言、写作风格和权威框架的提示条件。评估18个前沿LLM，我们发现超过一半的响应是不安全或部分不安全的（53.6%），而14.7%的内容包含严格有害的信息，观察到的最强效应不是从英语切换到孟加拉语，而是在孟加拉语中选择写作风格：同样的有害请求以正式的报纸调查形式表达时成功率比以随意消息形式表达时高出17个百分点，且没有涉及对抗性工程。我们进一步表明，现有的安全分类器在可靠评估孟加拉内容方面存在困难，甚至前沿模型在近一半的案例中也未能成功。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the gap in large language model (LLM) safety evaluation for Bengali, the seventh-most-spoken language, which has been largely overlooked in favor of English-centric assessments. Previous methods have primarily focused on English, leading to inadequate safety evaluations for Bengali content. The proposed approach, BanglaSafe, introduces a benchmark of 879 Bengali prompts that encompass various culturally grounded harm categories and prompting conditions, effectively addressing the limitations of existing methods. The study evaluates 18 leading LLMs and reveals that over half of the responses (53.6%) are unsafe or partially unsafe, with 14.7% containing strictly harmful content. Notably, the choice of writing style significantly impacts safety outcomes, demonstrating that formal requests are more likely to elicit safe responses compared to casual phrasing, highlighting the need for culturally aware safety assessments in LLMs.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估中的关键缺口，尽管孟加拉语是全球第七大语言，但安全评估主要集中在英语上。以往的方法在很大程度上忽视了文化特定的危害，并未充分评估LLM在孟加拉语中的响应，导致安全评估不可靠。所提出的方法BanglaSafe引入了一个包含879个孟加拉语提示的基准，涵盖了各种文化根植的危害类别和提示条件，从而提供了更全面的评估框架。该方法论涉及对18个领先的LLM进行评估，结果显示超过一半的响应是不安全或部分不安全的，安全性在很大程度上取决于写作风格而非语言本身。该研究为理解非英语环境中的LLM安全性做出了贡献，并突显了现有安全分类器在评估孟加拉语内容方面的不足。
Redteaming Leading Arabic LLMs with ASAS
Authors: Fidaa Abed, Haidar Khan, M Saiful Bari, Babar Khan, Abdalghani Abujabal
First: 2026-08-22T14:34:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-22T14:34:08+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figues, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As the adoption of large language models (LLMs) grows in Arabic-speaking regions, ensuring their safety and cultural alignment is increasingly critical. However, Arabic LLM safety remains underexplored, especially in adversarial evaluation settings. We introduce the Arabic Safety Index (ASAS), the first fully human-curated Arabic benchmark for redteaming LLMs. ASAS contains 801 prompts spanning 8 safety categories and 8 attack strategies, with ideal responses in Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). We conduct a redteaming evaluation across seven leading models with Arabic capabilities, including GPT-4o, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, and regional models such as ALLaM and FANAR. Human annotators rate responses using a structured 4-point safety scale, revealing that most models fail to defend against 50% of unsafe prompts. Our findings highlight major safety gaps in high-harm categories such as weapons and illicit substances, with direct and obfuscation-based attacks proving most effective. The results also show that language alignment does not readily transfer across languages, and that automated safety judges (e.g., GPT-4o) perform poorly compared to human annotators. ASAS provides a culturally grounded benchmark and redteaming protocol to drive progress in Arabic LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用ASAS对领先的阿拉伯语LLM进行红队测试
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在阿拉伯语地区的应用不断增长，确保其安全性和文化一致性变得愈发重要。然而，阿拉伯语LLM的安全性仍然未得到充分探索，尤其是在对抗性评估环境中。我们引入了阿拉伯安全指数（ASAS），这是第一个完全由人工策划的阿拉伯基准，用于对LLMs进行红队测试。ASAS包含801个提示，涵盖8个安全类别和8种攻击策略，并提供现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）的理想响应。我们对七个具有阿拉伯语能力的领先模型进行了红队评估，包括GPT-4o、Claude 3.7 Sonnet以及区域模型如ALLaM和FANAR。人类注释者使用结构化的4分安全评分标准对响应进行评分，结果显示大多数模型未能抵御50%的不安全提示。我们的研究结果突显了在武器和非法物质等高危类别中的主要安全缺口，直接攻击和混淆攻击被证明是最有效的。结果还表明，语言一致性并不能轻易跨语言转移，自动安全评估者（例如GPT-4o）的表现远不及人类注释者。ASAS提供了一个文化基础的基准和红队测试协议，以推动阿拉伯语LLM安全性的进展。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for safety and cultural alignment in Arabic large language models (LLMs), which have seen increased adoption but lack thorough adversarial evaluation. Previous methods for assessing LLM safety have been limited, often neglecting the unique challenges posed by the Arabic language and culture. The proposed approach introduces the Arabic Safety Index (ASAS), a comprehensive benchmark that includes 801 prompts across various safety categories and attack strategies, specifically designed for redteaming LLMs. This methodology allows for a structured evaluation of leading Arabic models, revealing significant safety vulnerabilities, particularly in high-risk areas like weapons and illicit substances, where over half of the prompts were inadequately handled. The findings underscore the necessity for culturally relevant safety assessments and demonstrate that existing automated safety judges are less effective than human evaluators, thereby contributing a valuable resource for enhancing the safety of Arabic LLMs.
本研究关注阿拉伯语地区大型语言模型（LLMs）在安全性和文化一致性方面的关键需求，这一领域在对抗性评估中被严重忽视。以往的方法未能充分评估阿拉伯LLMs的安全性，导致对其脆弱性的理解存在重大缺口。所提出的方法引入了阿拉伯安全指数（ASAS），这是一个全面的基准，包含801个跨多个安全类别和攻击策略的提示，从而允许对LLMs进行更彻底的评估。该方法论涉及人类评审员对模型响应进行结构化安全评分，结果显示许多领先模型未能处理大量不安全提示，尤其是在高风险领域。这些发现强调了改善阿拉伯LLMs安全性措施的必要性，并证明ASAS能够有效识别和解决这些安全问题，为该领域提供了有价值的见解。
CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Chengxiao Wang, Enyi Jiang, Xiaojing Liao, Sanmi Koyejo
First: 2026-08-21T16:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T16:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLEAR：用于保持效用的LLM安全对齐的连续潜在适配器路由
提高大型语言模型（LLM）的安全性往往会以效用为代价，因为全局应用的安全调优可能会影响模型对有害和良性输入的响应。我们提出了\textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing（CLEAR），这是一种条件安全适应框架，使用轻量级的隐藏状态门控来持续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度。CLEAR旨在减少有害的完成，同时避免对冻结主干的不必要更改，这可能会降低对良性提示的性能。对广泛使用的安全和效用基准的实验表明，CLEAR在HarmBench上提高了鲁棒性，同时减少了全局应用安全调优（如SFT或标准低秩适配（LoRA））所观察到的效用下降。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench ASR从32.3\%降低到0.5\%，同时保留了大部分基础模型的效用，并在GSM8K准确率上比全局应用的SFT或LoRA高出多达7.1个百分点。这些结果表明，CLEAR是改善LLM对齐中安全与效用权衡的有希望的机制。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) without compromising their utility, as traditional methods like globally applied safety tuning can negatively impact model responses. The proposed Continuous Latent Adapter Routing (CLEAR) framework introduces a lightweight hidden-state gate that allows for continuous control over the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter, thereby minimizing harmful outputs while preserving performance on benign inputs. This approach is well-motivated as it seeks to enhance the safety-utility balance in LLM alignment. The methodology involves using CLEAR to adaptively manage safety responses during model inference. Experimental results demonstrate that CLEAR significantly reduces harmful completions on the HarmBench benchmark from 32.3% to 0.5% and achieves a 7.1 percentage point improvement in GSM8K accuracy compared to traditional methods, indicating its effectiveness in maintaining utility while enhancing safety.
本研究解决了在不损害大型语言模型（LLMs）实用性的情况下提高其安全性的问题，因为传统方法如全球应用的安全调优可能会对模型响应产生负面影响。提出的连续潜在适配器路由（CLEAR）框架引入了一种轻量级的隐藏状态门，允许对安全低秩适配器的激活强度进行持续控制，从而减轻有害输出，同时保持对良性输入的性能。该方法的动机明确，因为它旨在平衡安全性和实用性，这在以往方法中一直是一个重大问题。该方法涉及动态调整模型的安全特性，实验表明CLEAR在HarmBench基准上显著减少了有害完成，同时提高了实用性指标，将HarmBench ASR从32.3%降低到0.5%，并在GSM8K准确率上比现有技术提高了多达7.1个百分点。这些发现表明CLEAR有效改善了LLM对齐中的安全性与实用性权衡。
RouteScan: A Non-Intrusive Approach to Auditing MoE LLMs Safety via Expert Routing Telemetry
Authors: Bo Lv, Zhiheng Xu, KeDong Xiu, Ruyi Ding, Tianhang Zheng, Zhibo Wang, Kui Ren
First: 2026-05-24T02:06:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T12:55:57+00:00
Comments: 11 pages. Revised manuscript with expanded experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), safety auditing becomes necessary to verify whether these models produce or facilitate harmful behaviors during operation. However, existing content-based auditing methods typically require access to user prompts, model internals, or outputs, potentially exposing sensitive user information and creating a tension between LLM safety and user privacy. On the other hand, we observe that, in MoE models, different inputs induce different sparse expert-routing patterns, which produce measurable footprints in low-level GPU execution telemetry. We refer to these hardware-observable signals induced by expert-routing decisions as expert routing telemetry; they are derived from GPU execution rather than from router logits or token-level routing assignments. Inspired by this observation, we propose RouteScan, a non-intrusive auditing framework for detecting harmful behaviors through such routing-induced GPU telemetry. Specifically, RouteScan utilizes the number of active GPU threads allocated to expert modules during the prefilling phase as a discriminative micro-architectural fingerprint, and builds a lightweight detection pipeline that isolates cross-domain invariant risk indicators for the precise identification of malicious prompts. Comprehensive evaluations on four open-source MoE LLMs with distinct routing designs demonstrate that RouteScan achieves strong generalization, with an AUROC exceeding 0.91 on unseen harmful domains. Moreover, privacy stress tests show that, although aggregated execution telemetry retains input-related attribute information, full prompts and exact sensitive fields cannot be reliably recovered under the evaluated attacks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RouteScan：一种通过专家路由遥测对MoE LLM安全性进行非侵入性审计的方法
随着混合专家（MoE）架构在大规模语言模型（LLM）中的日益采用，安全审计变得必要，以验证这些模型在操作过程中是否产生或促进有害行为。然而，现有的基于内容的审计方法通常需要访问用户提示、模型内部或输出，可能会暴露敏感用户信息，并在LLM安全性与用户隐私之间产生紧张关系。另一方面，我们观察到，在MoE模型中，不同的输入会引发不同的稀疏专家路由模式，这在低级GPU执行遥测中产生可测量的足迹。我们将这些由专家路由决策引发的硬件可观察信号称为专家路由遥测；它们源自GPU执行，而非路由器logits或令牌级路由分配。受此观察启发，我们提出了RouteScan，一种通过这种路由引发的GPU遥测检测有害行为的非侵入性审计框架。具体而言，RouteScan利用在预填充阶段分配给专家模块的活跃GPU线程数量作为区分性微架构指纹，并构建一个轻量级检测管道，隔离跨域不变风险指标，以精确识别恶意提示。对四个具有不同路由设计的开源MoE LLM的全面评估表明，RouteScan实现了强大的泛化能力，在未见的有害领域中AUROC超过0.91。此外，隐私压力测试表明，尽管聚合的执行遥测保留了与输入相关的属性信息，但在评估的攻击下，无法可靠地恢复完整提示和确切的敏感字段。

The proposed approach, RouteScan, differs from traditional methods by utilizing expert routing telemetry derived from GPU execution rather than user prompts or model outputs, thus avoiding privacy concerns. Certified Multi-Turn Robustness for LLM Safety via Compositional Bounds and Safety Persistence
Authors: Yang Liu, Bin Chong, Wenkai Yang, Shuai Zhang, Yancheng Chen, Feiyu Han, GuoZhen, Cheng Zhang, Huaibing Xie, Changze Lv, Shihan Dou, Pluto Zhou
First: 2026-08-21T07:43:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T07:43:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to multi-turn jailbreak attacks that progressively manipulate conversation context. Existing certified robustness methods are limited to single-turn inputs; naive multi-turn composition yields bounds that degrade exponentially in the number of turns. We introduce Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), a framework that models conversational safety via State-Adversarial MDPs and defines $k$-turn certified robustness as the worst-case safety probability across $k$ adversarial turns. MTCR comprises: (i) compositional certification via embedding-space mode decomposition, yielding tighter certified lower bounds than naive multiplication; (ii) $(α,β)$-safety persistence, improving the degradation rate from $\underline{p}^{k}$ to $β^k$ (with $β> \underline{p}$) and yielding interpretable horizon estimates; (iii) matching information-theoretic upper bounds establishing tightness; and (iv) a unified algorithm combining these results. Experiments on six LLMs under $ε$-bounded and Crescendo-style attacks confirm that empirical safety consistently exceeds the certified bounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过组合界限和安全持久性实现LLM安全的认证多轮鲁棒性
大型语言模型（LLMs）易受多轮越狱攻击，这些攻击逐步操控对话上下文。现有的认证鲁棒性方法仅限于单轮输入；简单的多轮组合导致界限在轮数上呈指数下降。我们引入了多轮认证鲁棒性（MTCR），这是一个通过状态对抗MDP建模对话安全的框架，并将$k$轮认证鲁棒性定义为$k$轮对抗中的最坏情况安全概率。MTCR包括：（i）通过嵌入空间模式分解进行组合认证，产生比简单乘法更紧的认证下界；（ii）$(α,β)$-安全持久性，将降级率从$\underline{p}^{k}$改善到$β^k$（其中$β> \underline{p}$），并产生可解释的时间估计；（iii）匹配信息论上界限以建立紧密性；（iv）结合这些结果的统一算法。在$ε$-有界和Crescendo风格攻击下对六个LLM的实验确认，经验安全性始终超过认证界限。

The proposed Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR) framework differentiates itself by utilizing State-Adversarial MDPs to define certified robustness across multiple turns, incorporating compositional certification for tighter bounds, and improving safety persistence to mitigate degradation rates.
